`obj_log`:
```
a = softmax(x)
conv = fft_autocorr(a, L)  # length 2n-1
vals = conv[mask]
m = vals.max()
shifted = vals - m
weights = exp(shifted/tau)
s = weights.sum()
if s <=0: return m
return m + tau*log(s)
```
This computes conv each call. We can cache? minimize calls same x repeatedly? not. Could store in global? not thread safe. FFT O(n log n) okay.

Need robust `project_simplex(v, s=1)`.
Algorithm: sort v descending; find rho such that v - τ >0. Use binary search or cumulative. Top-level:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, len(u)+1) > (cssv - 1))[0][-1]
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1) / (rho + 1.0)
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    ss = out.sum()
    if ss > 0: out /= ss  # not necessary if theta correct; due roundoff
    return out
```
Works for target sum1. If v all -inf? no.

```
if s <= 1e-300: return m
return m + tau*np.log(s)
```

```
Let's outline code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

RNG_SEED = 12345

def next_pow2(m): ...

def make_state_lin(n, mode): # mode 'full' or 'off'
    conv_len = 2*n-1
    L=next_pow2(conv_len)
    if mode=='full':
       idx=np.arange(conv_len)
    else:
       idx=np.concatenate([np.arange(0,n-1), np.arange(n, conv_len)]) # exclude n-1
    return (L,n,conv_len,idx)

def make_state_circ(n):
    L=n; conv_len=n; idx=np.arange(1,n); return (L,n,conv_len,idx)

def softmax_log(x): ...

def fft_conv(state, a): # compute conv values length conv_len
    L,n,conv_len, idx = state
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(A*A, n=L)[:conv_len]
    # For small negative, clip? If L<n? For linear L large; roundoff can negative. `np.maximum(conv,0.0,out=conv)`? If circular and signed? a nonnegative, C nonnegative. Clip.
    return conv

def exact_score_fast(a, state):
    L,n,conv_len,idx=state
    c=fft_conv(state,a)
    if len(idx)==0: return 0.0
    m=c[idx].max()
    S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-15: return inf
    return float(2*n*max(m,0.0)/(S*S))
```
But if mode circular, L=n, conv circular length n; score formula uses 2*n; okay.
```

```
`next_pow2(m)`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(np.ceil(np.log2(m))))
```

```

conv=fast_conv(state,a)
vals=conv[idx]
m=vals.max()
if m<=1e-300: return 0.0
shift=vals-m
shift=np.minimum(shift,0.0)
# To avoid underflow all zero, if max shift/tau < -745: return m
e=np.exp(shift/tau)
s=e.sum()
if s<=1e-300: return m
return m + tau*np.log(s)
```

```
def normalize_pos(a):
    a=np.maximum(np.asarray(a,float),0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-15: return np.ones(len(a))/len(a)
    return a/s
```

Resampling parent to n: If parent length !=n, use `resample_pos(parent, n)`. Implement:
```
def resample_pos(a, m):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=len(a)
    if m==n: return a
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,m)
    b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_pos(b)
```
Could introduce smoothing. Good.
```

def polish_exact(a, state, deadline, rng, use_eval, eval_before, eval_every_s=30, print_every=100):

       P=min(80,len(order))

       cmax=cact[0]
       if cmax <= 1e-30: break

```
fast score function:
```
def fast_score_by_mode(a, mode):
  a=np.asarray(a,float); n=len(a)
  S=a.sum()
  if S<1e-15: return inf
  if mode=='circ_off': state=make_state_circ(n)
  elif mode=='full_lin': state=make_state_lin(n,'full')
  else: state=make_state_lin(n,'off')
  return exact_score_fast(normalize_pos(a), state)

def choose_mode():
    rng=np.random.default_rng(777)
    n_cal=160
    seqs=[]
    seqs.append(np.ones(n_cal)/n_cal)
    seqs.append(rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n_cal)*0.7))
    seqs.append(rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n_cal)*2.0))
    # alternating
    x=1+0.7*np.cos(np.pi*np.arange(n_cal)); seqs.append(normalize_pos(x))
    # sine
    x=1+0.6*np.cos(2*np.pi*5*np.arange(n_cal)/n_cal)+0.3*np.cos(2*np.pi*11*np.arange(n_cal)/n_cal); seqs.append(normalize_pos(x))
    # parent if in globals
    p=globals().get('height_sequence_1')
    if p is not None: seqs.append(normalize_pos(np.asarray(p,float)))
    modes=['off_lin','full_lin','circ_off']
    errors={m:0.0 for m in modes}; counts={m:0}
    min_true=inf
    for seq in seqs:
       if len(seq)<2: continue
       try: true=evaluate_sequence(seq.tolist())
       except Exception: true=inf
       if not finite: continue
       min_true=min(min_true,true)
       for m in modes:

          if finite and fast>1e-12:
             ratio=true/fast
             if ratio>1e-12:
                errors[m]+=abs(np.log(ratio)); counts[m]+=1
    for m: if counts>0 errors/=counts
    # prefer off if min_true<1.9

       # among off modes choose smaller error

    if errors['full_lin'] < errors['off_lin'] - 0.1 and errors['full_lin'] < 0.1:
       return 'full_lin'
    # default off_lin unless circ clearly better
    if errors['circ_off'] + 0.05 < errors['off_lin']:
       return 'circ_off'
    return 'off_lin'

```
Primal: given f normalized sum=1, M = max_k c_k (or score constant). Solve max 1^T g s.t. (f*g)_k ≤ M for all k, g≥0. Then rescale g to sum1 and update f ← (1-t)f+t g. This LP uses f linear. It is a max-flow-like convolution constraint.
```

```
`lse_value`:
```
def lse_value(a, state, tau):
    conv=fast_conv(state,a)
    vals=conv[idx] # all maybe state idx; if full idx all but can avoid slicing? For full, conv directly. But state idx may be array; okay.
    m=vals.max()
    if m<=0: return 0
    shift=np.minimum(vals-m,0.0)
    # if tau extremely small, use top
    q=shift/tau
    # To avoid underflow, find max q=0; if min q < -745, okay exp zeros.
    e=np.exp(q)
    s=e.sum()
    if s==0: return m
    return m + tau*np.log(s)
```
If full idx all, conv length 2n-1. For memory, `vals` copy. okay.

`log_gradient_from_a`:
```
def log_gradient_from_a(a, state, tau):
    conv=fast_conv(...)
    vals=conv[idx]
    m=vals.max(); shift=...
    e=np.exp(shift/tau); s=e.sum()
    if s==0:
       # uniform on maxs
       w = (vals >= m - 1e-15) ; w=w/sum
    else w=e/s
    wfull=np.zeros(L)
    wfull[idx]=w
    # a_rev
    a_rev=np.zeros(L)
    if n>0: a_rev[0]=a[0]
    if n>1:
        if L >= 2*n-1:
            a_rev[-(n-1):] = a[1:][::-1]
        else: # circular L=n
            a_rev[1:] = a[:-1][::-1]
    Wr=np.fft.rfft(wfull,L); Ar=np.fft.rfft(a_rev,L)
    prod=Wr*Ar; prod[0]=prod[0].real; if L%2==0: prod[-1]=prod[-1].real
    grad=2.0*np.fft.irfft(prod,n=L)[:n]
    dot=np.dot(a, grad)
    gl=a*(grad-dot)
    return nan_to_num(gl)
```
For full idx array length 9611; wfull setting maybe copying. Could optimize by precomputing `idx` and if full use `wfull[:2n-1]=w` directly. State can include a flag? We'll just use assign; okay.

But `idx` for full could be `None` to avoid copy. To simplify, state tuple `(L,n,conv_len,mask_idx)`. If mask_idx equals all, assignment still. Fine.

`fast_conv`:
```
def fast_conv(state,a):
 L,n,conv_len,mask_idx=state
 A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
 spec=A*A
 spec[0]=spec[0].real
 if L%2==0: spec[-1]=spec[-1].real
 c=np.fft.irfft(spec,n=L)[:conv_len]
 np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
 return c
```
If conv_len < L (full linear conv_len=2n-1, L maybe power >). Good.

A small issue: For linear convolution with L power >2n-1, `irfft` returns length L. Coefficients 0..2n-2 correct; remaining zeros (roundoff). Good.

```

w=np.ones(P)/P
H=(H+H.T)/2
# add ridge proportional trace
ridge = 1e-12*max(1.0, np.trace(H)/P)
H += ridge*np.eye(P)

eta=1.0/L
for _ in range(100):
   grad=2*H@w
   w_new=project_simplex(w - eta*grad)
   if np.max(np.abs(w_new-w))<1e-8: w=w_new; break
   w=w_new
return w
```

Now, performance of `project_simplex`: For large v, sorting O(n log n). In line search, many. We can implement faster using binary search O(n) to reduce. Let's write O(n) projection:
Algorithm for simplex sum=1:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v,float)
    u = np.copy(v)
    # find threshold theta via binary search over min/max
    lo = v.min() - 1
    hi = v.max()
    for _ in range(40):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.maximum(v-mid,0).sum()
       if s > 1: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    theta=(lo+hi)/2
    out=np.maximum(v-theta,0)
    s=out.sum()
    if s>0: out/=s
    return out
```
But if target sum1, the median condition. Use 50 iterations O(n*50) worse than sort sometimes. Sorting in C fast. Keep sorting. `project_simplex` with sorting uses accumulate; fast.

Need handle empty? no.
```

```
`next_pow2`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << int(np.ceil(np.log2(float(m))))
```

`make_state_lin`:
```
def make_state_lin(n):
    conv_len=2*n-1
    L=next_pow2(conv_len)
    idx=np.arange(conv_len, dtype=np.int64)
    return (L,n,conv_len,idx)
```
Only full.

`fast_conv` as above.

`lse_value`:
```
def lse_value(a,state,tau):
    L,n,conv_len,idx=state
    c=fast_conv(state,a)
    vals=c[idx]
    m=float(np.max(vals)) if len(idx)>0 else 0.0
    if m <= 1e-300: return 0.0
    if tau <= 1e-300:
        return m
    shift=vals-m
    shift=np.minimum(shift,0.0)
    q=shift/tau
    # If q too negative, exp zeros; at least max element q=0.
    e=np.exp(q)
    s=e.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
       # fallback exact max
       return m
    return float(m + tau*np.log(s))
```
Since idx all, `vals` copy. Could avoid copy by `vals=c` if full. We'll set if idx is None? Let's store `idx` as None for full to optimize. `make_state_lin` returns idx=None, and functions check if idx is None: vals=conv else conv[idx]. This is easy.
Modify state `(L,n,conv_len,idx)` idx optional.
In `fast_conv`, no idx.
`lse_value`: `vals = c if idx is None else c[idx]`.
`log_gradient_from_a`: weights length = len(vals); wfull assignment: if idx is None: `wfull[:conv_len] = w` else `wfull[idx]=w`. Good.

`make_state_lin`: idx=None.

`a_rev` construction:
```
def make_a_rev(a,L,n):
    ar=np.zeros(L)
    if n>0:
       ar[0]=a[0]
       if n>1:
          # For linear full, L>=2n-1; for safety if L>=2n-1 use end assignment; else circular?
          if L >= 2*n-1:
             ar[-(n-1):] = a[1:][::-1]
          else:
             # fallback: circular maybe, but we don't use
             rev = a[1:][::-1]
             ar[1:1+len(rev)] = rev[:L-1]
    return ar
```
Since only linear full, L>=2n-1. Good.

`log_gradient_from_a`:
```
def log_gradient_from_a(a,state,tau):
 L,n,conv_len,idx=state
 c=fast_conv(state,a)
 vals = c if idx is None else c[idx]
 m=np.max(vals)
 if m<=1e-300: return np.zeros_like(a)
 shift=np.minimum(vals-m,0.0)
 if tau<=1e-300:
    w=np.where(np.isclose(vals,m,rtol=1e-12,atol=1e-300),1.0,0.0)
    s=w.sum(); if s==0: w=...; w/=s
 else:
    e=np.exp(shift/tau); s=e.sum()
    if s<=1e-300:
       w=np.where(np.isnan? vals >= m - 745*tau? )...
    else: w=e/s
 # In case underflow all except maybe max: if s==0, w uniform over vals >= m - 1e-12*max(1,m)
```
Simplify: if `tau very small`, `q=shift/tau`; q for max 0, e>=1, sum>=1, no underflow all. Actually if difference > 745*tau, exp underflows to 0, but max elements remain 1. So sum at least number exact max. Good. No need fallback unless rounding vals-m positive? shift min 0, max shift=0, exp(0)=1. So s>=1. Good.
```
wfull=np.zeros(L)
if idx is None: wfull[:conv_len]=w
else: wfull[idx]=w
ar=make_a_rev(a,L,n)
Wr=np.fft.rfft(wfull,L); Ar=np.fft.rfft(ar,L)
prod=Wr*Ar; prod[0]=prod[0].real; if L%2==0: prod[-1]=prod[-1].real
grad=2.0*np.fft.irfft(prod,n=L)[:n]
dot=np.dot(a,grad)

return np.nan_to_num(gl, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

```
`exact_score_fast`:
```

def exact_score_fast(a,state):
 L,n,conv_len,idx=state
 c=fast_conv(state,a)
 vals=c if idx is None else c[idx]
 if len(vals)==0: return 0.0
 m=float(np.max(vals)); S=float(np.sum(a))
 if S<1e-15: return float('inf')
 return float(2.0*n*m/(S*S))

```
`softmax_log`:
```

def softmax_log(x):

 y=np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
 y=np.clip(y, -700.0, 700.0) # before max? If large, okay
 m=np.max(y)
 y=y-m
 y=np.clip(y, -700.0, 0.0)
 e=np.exp(y)
 s=e.sum()
 if s<=1e-300: return np.ones(len(e))/len(e)
 return e/s
```

`make_smooth_basis(n,M)`:
```
def make_smooth_basis(n,M):
 x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
 basis=np.empty((2*M,n), dtype=np.float64)
 row=0
 for m in range(1,M+1):
    basis[row,:]=np.cos(np.pi*m*x); row+=1
    if 2*M>row: basis[row,:]=np.sin(np.pi*m*x); row+=1
 return basis
```
If we want only 2M, okay. For M=1, includes cosπ,sinπ.
```

def theta_from_a(a,basis):

   coeff,_,_,_=np.linalg.lstsq(basis, loga, rcond=None)
   return np.asarray(coeff,float)
 except Exception:
   return np.zeros(basis.shape[0])

def smooth_optimize(theta0,state,basis,time_limit):

```
`project_simplex` as sort.
```
def project_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    v=np.where(np.isfinite(v),v,0.0)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    cond = u * np.arange(1, len(u)+1) > (cssv - 1.0)
    rho=np.nonzero(cond)[0]
    if len(rho)==0:
       out=np.zeros_like(v); out[0]=1.0; return out
    r=rho[-1]
    theta=(cssv[r]-1.0)/(r+1.0)
    out=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    s=out.sum()
    if s>0: out/=s
    return out
```
Works for target sum 1. Need if v all very negative, cond maybe? If u all negative, cssv -1 negative, cond maybe? Let's test v=[-1,-1], u[-1,-1], cssv[-2,-4], u*idx: -1,-2 > cssv-1: -3,-5 -> true true, rho last=1, theta=(cssv[1]-1)/2=(-4-1)/2=-2.5, out=max(v+2.5)=1.5 each,sum3? Wait if all negative, projection should uniform [0.5,0.5]. Algo standard: if no rho? Actually condition `u * np.arange(1,n+1) > (cssv - 1)`. For u=-1,-1: idx* u = -1,-2; cssv-1 = -3,-5; condition true -> rho=1. theta=(cssv[1]-1)/(2)=(-4-1)/2=-2.5; v-theta =1.5,1.5, sum3 not 1. Because standard algorithm assumes target sum=1 and v can be negative? It works: theta = (cssv[rho]-1)/(rho+1) = (-4-1)/2=-2.5. Then out = max(v-theta,0) = max(1.5)= both, sum3. But theta should be? Let's derive: We need ∑max(v_i-θ,0)=1. If all selected rho=2, equation ∑(v_i-θ)=n (sum v - nθ)=1 => θ=(sumv -1)/n=(-2-1)/2=-1.5. Standard formula with 1-index: theta=(cssv[rho]-1)/(rho+1)? For u sorted, if rho 1 (0-index), rho+1=2, cssv[1]=-2, theta=(-2-1)/2=-1.5. I mistakenly used -4? Wait cssv[1] for u[-1,-1] is -2? Actually u = [-1,-1], cssv = [-2,-4]? No cumsum of [-1,-1] is [-1,-2]. Sorry. theta=(-2-1)/2=-1.5, out0.5 each. Good.
If all v=0, u=[0,0], cssv[1]=-1, condition 0>-1 true, theta=(-1-1)/2=-1, out1 each sum2? Wait projection of zero to simplex should uniform 0.5. Equation sum v=0, θ=(0-1)/2=-0.5. Standard formula for cssv[rho] with rho last? For v=[0,0], u*idx [0,0], cssv=[0,0]? Cumsum of [0,0] is [0,0]. condition 0 > -1 true; rho=1; theta=(cssv[1]-1)/(2)=(-1)/2=-0.5; out0.5. Good. I confused. Works.
```
Need if len(rho)=0, return uniform.

`grad_single_lin` maybe not needed if build G direct. We'll write `build_active_gradients(active,a,n)` top-level.
```
def build_active_gradients(active,a,n):
    P=len(active)
    G=np.zeros((n,P), dtype=np.float64)
    for col,k in enumerate(active):
        if k <= n-1:
            r=k+1
            G[:r,col]=2.0*a[:r][::-1]
        else:
            start=k-(n-1)
            r=2*n-1-k
            if r>0:
                G[start:start+r,col]=2.0*a[start:n][::-1]
    return G
```
Check for k=2n-2: start=n-1, r=1, G[n-1]=2*a[n-1]. Good. For k=n-1: start=0,r=n, G[:n]=2*a[:n][::-1]. Valid central.

`min_norm_weights(H)`:
```
def min_norm_weights(H):
    P=H.shape[0]
    w=np.ones(P)/P
    H=np.asarray(H,float)
    H=(H+H.T)*0.5
    trace=float(np.trace(H))
    ridge=1e-12*max(1.0, trace/max(1,P))
    H=H + ridge*np.eye(P)
    try:
       L=2.0*(float(np.linalg.norm(H,2)) + ridge)
    except Exception:
       L=1.0
    eta=1.0/max(1e-300,L)
    for _ in range(80):
       grad=2.0*H.dot(w)
       w_new=project_simplex(w - eta*grad)
       if np.max(np.abs(w_new-w)) < 1e-8:
          w=w_new; break
       w=w_new
    w=np.maximum(w,0); s=w.sum(); if s>0: w/=s
    return w
```
Good.

```
def polish_exact(a_in,state,deadline,rng,use_eval,eval_interval=20.0):
    n=state[1]
    a=normalize_pos(a_in)
    best_a=a.copy()
    best_score=exact_score_fast(a,state)
    best_true_a=None; best_true_score=inf
    last_eval=time.monotonic()-eval_interval
    alpha_scale=1.0
    stall=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c=fast_conv(state,best_a)
        vals=c  # full
        order=np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
        P=min(40, len(order))
        active=order[:P].astype(np.int64)
        cact=vals[active]
        cmax=float(cact[0])
        if cmax <= 1e-300: break
        # If top gap all equal? H zero? 
        G=build_active_gradients(active,best_a,n)
        H=G.T@G
        w=min_norm_weights(H)
        g=G.dot(w)
        # If direction norm tiny, large random?
        if np.linalg.norm(g) < 1e-20:
            break
        a_mean=float(np.mean(best_a)); g_mean=float(np.mean(np.abs(g)))
        base=0.2*a_mean/max(g_mean,1e-300)
        base=max(1e-4, min(1.0, base*alpha_scale))
        alphas=base*np.array([2.0,1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125,0.015625,0.0078125])
        improved=False
        for alpha in alphas:
            cand=project_simplex(best_a - alpha*g)

                best_score=sc; best_a=cand; improved=True

        # Instead of break, choose best candidate:
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing

    score(a) = 2 * n * max_k (a * a)_k / (sum(a))^2

where * is linear convolution.

The code uses a hybrid strategy:
1. A low-dimensional smooth-parameterized optimization phase. Sequences are
   written as a normalized exponent of a finite trigonometric polynomial,
   which is effective for finding good continuous density shapes.
2. A full-parameter L-BFGS phase in log-space with a log-sum-exp smoothing
   of the max convolution coefficient.
3. An exact max-coefficient polishing phase using active-set subgradient
   directions, steepest-descerting subgradient weights, and projected line
   searches.

All candidates are selected and validated against the provided
evaluate_sequence() when available.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << int(np.ceil(np.log2(float(m))))


def normalize_pos(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def make_state_lin(n):
    conv_len = max(1, 2 * n - 1)
    L = next_pow2(conv_len)
    return (L, n, conv_len, None)


def fast_conv(state, a):
    L, n, conv_len, _ = state
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    spec = A * A
    spec[0] = spec[0].real
    if L % 2 == 0:
        spec[-1] = spec[-1].real
    c = np.fft.irfft(spec, n=L)[:conv_len]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def exact_score_fast(a, state):
    L, n, conv_len, idx = state
    c = fast_conv(state, a)
    vals = c if idx is None else c[idx]
    if vals.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    m = float(np.max(vals))
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 1e-15:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (S * S))


def lse_value(a, state, tau):
    L, n, conv_len, idx = state
    c = fast_conv(state, a)
    vals = c if idx is None else c[idx]
    if vals.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    m = float(np.max(vals))
    if m <= 1e-300:
        return 0.0
    if tau <= 1e-300:
        return m
    shift = vals - m
    shift = np.minimum(shift, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(shift / tau)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return m
    return float(m + tau * np.log(s))


def make_a_rev(a, L, n):
    ar = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 0:
        ar[0] = a[0]
        if n > 1:
            # We only use zero-padded linear convolution, so L >= 2n-1.
            if L >= 2 * n - 1:
                ar[-(n - 1):] = a[1:][::-1]
            else:
                tail = a[1:][::-1]
                ar[1:1 + tail.size] = tail[:max(0, L - 1)]
    return ar


def log_gradient_from_a(a, state, tau):
    L, n, conv_len, idx = state
    c = fast_conv(state, a)
    vals = c if idx is None else c[idx]
    if vals.size == 0:
        return np.zeros_like(a)

    m = float(np.max(vals))
    if m <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros_like(a)

    shift = vals - m
    shift = np.minimum(shift, 0.0)

    if tau <= 1e-300:
        w = np.where(vals >= m - 1e-14 * max(1.0, m), 1.0, 0.0)
        s = float(w.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            w = np.ones_like(vals)
            s = float(w.size)
        w /= s
    else:
        e = np.exp(shift / tau)
        s = float(e.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            w = np.where(vals >= m - 745.0 * tau, 1.0, 0.0)
            s = float(w.sum())
            if s <= 0.0:
                w = np.zeros_like(vals)
                imax = int(np.argmax(vals))
                w[imax] = 1.0
                s = 1.0
            w /= s
        else:
            w = e / s

    wfull = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if idx is None:
        wfull[:conv_len] = w
    else:
        wfull[idx] = w

    ar = make_a_rev(a, L, n)
    W = np.fft.rfft(wfull, n=L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(ar, n=L)
    prod = W * A
    prod[0] = prod[0].real
    if L % 2 == 0:
        prod[-1] = prod[-1].real

    grad_a = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(prod, n=L)[:n]
    dot = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_log = a * (grad_a - dot)
    return np.nan_to_num(grad_log, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def softmax_log(x):
    y = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    y = np.clip(y, -700.0, 700.0)
    y = y - float(np.max(y))
    y = np.clip(y, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(y.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(y.size)
    return e / s


def obj_log(x, state, tau):
    a = softmax_log(x)
    return lse_value(a, state, tau)


def grad_log(x, state, tau):
    a = softmax_log(x)
    return log_gradient_from_a(a, state, tau)


def make_smooth_basis(n, M):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    basis = np.empty((2 * M, n), dtype=np.float64)
    row = 0
    for m in range(1, M + 1):
        basis[row, :] = np.cos(np.pi * m * x)
        row += 1
        basis[row, :] = np.sin(np.pi * m * x)
        row += 1
    return basis


def a_from_smooth(theta, basis):
    z = basis.dot(theta)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(z.size)
    return e / s


def obj_smooth(theta, state, basis, tau):
    a = a_from_smooth(theta, basis)
    return lse_value(a, state, tau)


def grad_smooth(theta, state, basis, tau):
    a = a_from_smooth(theta, basis)
    gl = log_gradient_from_a(a, state, tau)
    return basis.dot(gl)


def theta_from_a(a, basis):
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), 1e-300))
    try:
        coeff = np.linalg.lstsq(basis, loga, rcond=None)[0]
        coeff = np.asarray(coeff, dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(coeff)):
            return np.zeros(basis.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
        return coeff
    except Exception:
        return np.zeros(basis.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)


def manual_thetas(M, rng):
    thetas = []
    thetas.append(np.zeros(2 * M, dtype=np.float64))

    if M >= 1:
        t = np.zeros(2 * M, dtype=np.float64)
        t[0] = 1.6
        thetas.append(t.copy())

        t = np.zeros(2 * M, dtype=np.float64)
        t[0] = -1.6
        thetas.append(t.copy())

        t = np.zeros(2 * M, dtype=np.float64)
        t[1] = 1.6
        thetas.append(t.copy())

        t = np.zeros(2 * M, dtype=np.float64)
        t[1] = -1.6
        thetas.append(t.copy())

        t = np.zeros(2 * M, dtype=np.float64)
        t[0] = 1.2
        t[1] = 1.2
        thetas.append(t.copy())

        t = np.zeros(2 * M, dtype=np.float64)
        t[0] = -1.2
        t[1] = 1.2
        thetas.append(t.copy())

    for scale in (0.5, 1.0, 1.8):
        thetas.append(rng.normal(0.0, scale, 2 * M).astype(np.float64))

    return thetas


def smooth_optimize(theta0, state, basis, time_limit):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, time_limit)
    theta = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = a_from_smooth(theta, basis)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = exact_score_fast(a, state)

    c = fast_conv(state, a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if cmax < 1e-12:
        cmax = 1e-12

    if time_limit < 10.0:
        maxfun = 300
    elif time_limit < 30.0:
        maxfun = 800
    elif time_limit < 90.0:
        maxfun = 1500
    else:
        maxfun = 2500

    fractions = (0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125, 0.015625)

    for frac in fractions:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        tau = max(1e-14, float(frac) * cmax)
        opts = {
            "maxiter": max(100, maxfun),
            "maxfun": maxfun,
            "ftol": 1e-12,
            "gtol": 1e-8,
        }
        try:
            res = minimize(
                obj_smooth,
                theta,
                jac=grad_smooth,
                args=(state, basis, tau),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options=opts,
            )
            theta = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            theta = np.where(np.isfinite(theta), theta, 0.0)
        except Exception:
            break

        a = a_from_smooth(theta, basis)
        sc = exact_score_fast(a, state)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        c = fast_conv(state, a)
        cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if cmax < 1e-12:
            cmax = 1e-12

    return best_a, best_score, theta


def smooth_phase(n, budget, rng, warm_a):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, budget)
    state = make_state_lin(n)

    best_a = normalize_pos(warm_a)
    best_a = best_a.reshape(n) if best_a.size == n else np.ones(n) / float(n)
    best_score = exact_score_fast(best_a, state)

    kept_a = [best_a.copy()]
    kept_s = [best_score]

    print(f"smooth phase: n={n}, start={best_score:.8f}")

    base_ms = (8, 14, 22)
    for M0 in base_ms:
        M = int(min(M0, max(4, n // 4)))
        if M <= 0:
            continue
        basis = make_smooth_basis(n, M)
        starts = manual_thetas(M, rng)
        starts.append(theta_from_a(best_a, basis))
        # Also include projection of a few random warm starts only if time remains.
        total = len(starts)
        for i, theta in enumerate(starts):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
            time_i = remaining / max(1, total - i)
            if time_i < 6.0:
                break
            try:
                a, sc, theta = smooth_optimize(theta, state, basis, time_i)
            except Exception:
                continue
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
                continue
            sc = float(sc)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                kept_a.append(a.copy())
                kept_s.append(sc)
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                print(f"  M={M}, start={i + 1}/{total}, fast={sc:.8f}, best={best_score:.8f}")

    # Keep top smooth candidates.
    order = np.argsort(np.asarray(kept_s, dtype=np.float64))
    top = []
    for idx in order[:8]:
        top.append(kept_a[int(idx)].copy())

    return best_a, best_score, top


def optimize_log_lse_full(a0, state, time_limit):
    n = state[1]
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, time_limit)

    a = normalize_pos(a0)
    if a.size != n:
        a = resample_pos(a, n)

    x = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    x = x - float(np.mean(x))

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = exact_score_fast(a, state)

    c = fast_conv(state, a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 1.0 / max(1, 2 * n - 1)
    if cmax < 1e-12:
        cmax = 1e-12

    if time_limit < 20.0:
        maxfun = 300
    elif time_limit < 80.0:
        maxfun = 900
    else:
        maxfun = 2000

    fractions = (0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125, 0.015625, 0.0078125)

    for frac in fractions:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 4.0:
            break
        tau = max(1e-14, float(frac) * cmax)
        opts = {
            "maxiter": max(100, maxfun),
            "maxfun": maxfun,
            "ftol": 1e-12,
            "gtol": 1e-10,
        }
        try:
            res = minimize(
                obj_log,
                x,
                jac=grad_log,
                args=(state, tau),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options=opts,
            )
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
            x = x - float(np.mean(x))
        except Exception:
            break

        a = softmax_log(x)
        sc = exact_score_fast(a, state)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        c = fast_conv(state, a)
        cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 1.0
        if cmax < 1e-12:
            cmax = 1e-12

    return best_a, best_score


def run_full_phase(inits, state, budget):
    n = state[1]
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, budget)

    processed_a = []
    processed_s = []

    for arr in inits:
        if arr is None:
            continue
        a = normalize_pos(arr)
        if a.size != n:
            a = resample_pos(a, n)

        dup = False
        for b in processed_a:
            if np.linalg.norm(a - b, ord=1) < 1e-7:
                dup = True
                break
        if dup:
            continue

        sc = exact_score_fast(a, state)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            sc = float("inf")
        processed_a.append(a.copy())
        processed_s.append(float(sc))

    if not processed_a:
        best_a = np.ones(n) / float(n)
        return best_a, exact_score_fast(best_a, state)

    scores = np.asarray(processed_s, dtype=np.float64)
    order = np.argsort(scores)
    weights = np.ones(len(order), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(min(3, len(order))):
        weights[i] = 2.0

    best_a = processed_a[int(order[0])].copy()
    best_s = scores[int(order[0])]

    print(f"full L-BFGS phase: {len(processed_a)} inits, start best={best_s:.8f}")

    for pos, idx in enumerate(order):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
            break
        idx = int(idx)
        remaining_weight = float(weights[pos:].sum())
        if remaining_weight <= 0.0:
            break
        time_i = (deadline - time.monotonic()) * weights[pos] / remaining_weight
        time_i = max(8.0, min(time_i, 900.0))
        try:
            a_new, sc_new = optimize_log_lse_full(processed_a[idx], state, time_i)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_s:
            best_s = float(sc_new)
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            print(f"  full L-BFGS init {idx}: {best_s:.8f}")

    return best_a, best_s


def project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    n = v.size
    cond = u * np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64) > (cssv - 1.0)
    rho = np.nonzero(cond)[0]
    if rho.size == 0:
        out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        out[0] = 1.0
        return out
    r = int(rho[-1])
    theta = (cssv[r] - 1.0) / (r + 1.0)
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        out /= s
    return out


def true_score(a):
    try:
        vals = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
    if ev is None:
        return float("inf")
    try:
        s = ev(vals)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    try:
        s = float(s)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    return s if np.isfinite(s) else float("inf")


def build_active_gradients(active, a, n):
    P = len(active)
    G = np.zeros((n, P), dtype=np.float64)
    for col, k in enumerate(active):
        k = int(k)
        if k <= n - 1:
            r = k + 1
            G[:r, col] = 2.0 * a[:r][::-1]
        else:
            start = k - (n - 1)
            r = 2 * n - 1 - k
            if r > 0:
                G[start:start + r, col] = 2.0 * a[start:n][::-1]
    return G


def min_norm_weights(H):
    H = np.asarray(H, dtype=np.float64)
    P = H.shape[0]
    w = np.ones(P, dtype=np.float64) / float(P)

    H = 0.5 * (H + H.T)
    trace = float(np.trace(H))
    ridge = 1e-12 * max(1.0, trace / max(1, P))
    H = H + ridge * np.eye(P, dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        L = 2.0 * (float(np.linalg.norm(H, 2)) + ridge)
    except Exception:
        L = 1.0
    eta = 1.0 / max(1e-300, L)

    for _ in range(80):
        grad = 2.0 * H.dot(w)
        w_new = project_simplex(w - eta * grad)
        if np.max(np.abs(w_new - w)) < 1e-8:
            w = w_new
            break
        w = w_new

    w = np.maximum(w, 0.0)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        w /= s
    else:
        w = np.ones(P, dtype=np.float64) / float(P)
    return w


def polish_exact(a_in, state, deadline, rng, use_eval=True, eval_interval=20.0):
    n = state[1]
    a = normalize_pos(a_in)
    if a.size != n:
        a = resample_pos(a, n)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = exact_score_fast(best_a, state)

    best_true_a = best_a.copy()
    best_true_score = true_score(best_a) if use_eval else float("inf")

    last_eval = time.monotonic()
    alpha_scale = 1.0
    stall = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = fast_conv(state, best_a)
        if c.size == 0:
            break

        order = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
        P = min(32, int(order.size))
        active = order[:P].astype(np.int64, copy=False)
        cmax = float(c[active[0]]) if active.size else 0.0
        if cmax <= 1e-300:
            break

        G = build_active_gradients(active, best_a, n)
        H = G.T.dot(G)
        w = min_norm_weights(H)
        g = G.dot(w)
        g_norm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if g_norm < 1e-20:
            break

        a_mean = float(np.mean(best_a))
        g_mean = float(np.mean(np.abs(g)))
        base = 0.25 * a_mean / max(1e-300, g_mean)
        base = max(1e-5, min(1.0, base * alpha_scale))

        alphas = base * np.array(
            [2.0, 1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125, 0.015625], dtype=np.float64
        )

        improved = False
        for alpha in alphas:
            cand = project_simplex(best_a - float(alpha) * g)
            sc = exact_score_fast(cand, state)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-15:
                improved = True
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_a = cand

        if improved:
            stall = 0
            alpha_scale = min(1.5, alpha_scale * 1.05)
        else:
            stall += 1
            alpha_scale *= 0.7
            if stall > 60:
                sigma = 0.03 * max(1e-12, float(np.mean(best_a)))
                cand = project_simplex(best_a + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n).astype(np.float64))
                sc = exact_score_fast(cand, state)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = float(sc)
                    best_a = cand
                alpha_scale = 1.0
                stall = 0
                print(f"  polish reheat perturbation at fast={best_score:.8f}")

            if alpha_scale < 1e-4 and stall > 200:
                break

        if use_eval and np.isfinite(best_score) and (time.monotonic() - last_eval) > eval_interval:
            ts = true_score(best_a)
            if np.isfinite(ts) and ts < best_true_score:
                best_true_score = ts
                best_true_a = best_a.copy()
            last_eval = time.monotonic()

        if time.monotonic() - last_print > 30.0:
            last_print = time.monotonic()
            print(f"  polish fast={best_score:.8f}, true={best_true_score:.8f}")

    if use_eval:
        ts = true_score(best_a)
        if np.isfinite(ts) and ts < best_true_score:
            best_true_score = ts
            best_true_a = best_a.copy()

    return best_a, best_score, best_true_a, best_true_score


def resample_pos(a, m):
    a = normalize_pos(a)
    n = a.size
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        return a
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, max(1, n))
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_pos(b)


def fast_score_seq(a):
    a = normalize_pos(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return float("inf")
    state = make_state_lin(n)
    return exact_score_fast(a, state)


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_time = float(max(60.0, float(budget_s)))
    deadline = t_start + total_time - 60.0
    if deadline < t_start + 20.0:
        deadline = t_start + 30.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(1234567)

    # Warm start from known construction if present.
    parent = None
    pseq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if pseq is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(pseq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
            if arr.size >= 64 and float(arr.sum()) > 1e-8:
                parent = normalize_pos(arr)
        except Exception:
            parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        n = int(parent.size)
        if n < 128 or n > 12000:
            parent = resample_pos(parent, 4806)
            n = 4806
    else:
        n = 4806

    state = make_state_lin(n)
    uniform = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    best_a = uniform.copy()
    best_fast = exact_score_fast(best_a, state)
    best_true_a = uniform.copy()
    best_true_score = true_score(best_a)

    if parent is not None:
        parent = parent.reshape(n) if parent.size == n else resample_pos(parent, n)
        pfast = exact_score_fast(parent, state)
        ptrue = true_score(parent)
        if np.isfinite(pfast) and pfast < best_fast:
            best_fast = pfast
            best_a = parent.copy()
        if np.isfinite(ptrue) and ptrue < best_true_score:
            best_true_score = ptrue
            best_true_a = parent.copy()
        print(f"initial parent fast={best_fast:.8f}, true={best_true_score:.8f}")
    else:
        print(f"initial uniform fast={best_fast:.8f}, true={best_true_score:.8f}")

    # Phase 1: smooth low-dimensional search.
    smooth_alloc = min(750.0, max(60.0, 0.30 * total_time))
    smooth_spent = 0.0
    smooth_list = []
    if time.monotonic() + 30.0 < deadline:
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        try:
            smooth_a, smooth_score, smooth_list = smooth_phase(n, smooth_alloc, rng, best_a)
            if np.isfinite(smooth_score) and smooth_score < best_fast:
                best_fast = float(smooth_score)
                best_a = smooth_a
            ts = true_score(smooth_a)
            if np.isfinite(ts) and ts < best_true_score:
                best_true_score = ts
                best_true_a = smooth_a
            print(f"after smooth: best_fast={best_fast:.8f}, best_true={best_true_score:.8f}")
        except Exception as exc:
            print("smooth phase failed:", exc)
        smooth_spent = time.monotonic() - t0

    # Phase 2: full L-BFGS restarts.
    full_alloc = min(1050.0, max(90.0, 0.40 * total_time))
    full_alloc_eff = full_alloc + max(0.0, smooth_alloc - smooth_spent)
    remaining_for_full = deadline - time.monotonic() - 140.0
    full_budget = max(0.0, min(full_alloc_eff, remaining_for_full))

    if full_budget > 30.0:
        inits = []
        if parent is not None:
            inits.append(parent)
        inits.append(best_a)
        if smooth_list:
            inits.extend(smooth_list)

        # Add a few explicit shape seeds.
        try:
            x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            seeds = []
            seeds.append(1.0 + 0.8 * np.cos(np.pi * x))
            seeds.append(1.0 - 0.8 * np.cos(np.pi * x))
            seeds.append(1.0 + 0.8 * np.sin(np.pi * x))
            seeds.append(1.0 - 0.8 * np.sin(np.pi * x))
            seeds.append(1.0 + 0.7 * (0.5 - x))
            for s in seeds:
                s = np.maximum(s, 1e-6)
                inits.append(normalize_pos(s))
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            full_a, full_score = run_full_phase(inits, state, full_budget)
            if np.isfinite(full_score) and full_score < best_fast:
                best_fast = float(full_score)
                best_a = full_a
            ts = true_score(full_a)
            if np.isfinite(ts) and ts < best_true_score:
                best_true_score = ts
                best_true_a = full_a
            print(f"after full L-BFGS: best_fast={best_fast:.8f}, best_true={best_true_score:.8f}")
        except Exception as exc:
            print("full L-BFGS phase failed:", exc)

    # Phase 3: exact active-set polishing.
    polish_deadline = deadline - 30.0
    if time.monotonic() < polish_deadline - 30.0:
        try:
            p_a, p_fast, p_true_a, p_true = polish_exact(
                best_a,
                state,
                polish_deadline,
                rng,
                use_eval=True,
                eval_interval=max(10.0, min(30.0, total_time / 60.0)),
            )
            if np.isfinite(p_fast) and p_fast < best_fast:
                best_fast = float(p_fast)
                best_a = p_a
            if np.isfinite(p_true) and p_true < best_true_score:
                best_true_score = float(p_true)
                best_true_a = p_true_a
            print(f"after polish: best_fast={best_fast:.8f}, best_true={best_true_score:.8f}")
        except Exception as exc:
            print("polish phase failed:", exc)

    # Final candidate benchmarking and validation.
    candidates = []

    def add_candidate(arr):
        if arr is None:
            return
        try:
            b = normalize_pos(arr)
            if b.size >= 2:
                candidates.append(b)
        except Exception:
            pass

    add_candidate(best_a)
    add_candidate(best_true_a)
    add_candidate(parent)
    add_candidate(uniform)
    if smooth_list:
        for s in smooth_list:
            add_candidate(s)

    # Try some length variants of the best sequence.
    try:
        for factor in (0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0):
            m = int(round(n * factor))
            if 32 <= m <= 12000:
                add_candidate(resample_pos(best_a, m))
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Score candidates by fast proxy, then validate top ones.
    if candidates:
        fast_scores = np.empty(len(candidates), dtype=np.float64)
        for i, cand in enumerate(candidates):
            try:
                fs = fast_score_seq(cand)
            except Exception:
                fs = float("inf")
            fast_scores[i] = fs if np.isfinite(fs) else float("inf")
        order = np.argsort(fast_scores)

        evaluated = set()
        attempt_limit = int(min(36, max(6, total_time / 60.0)))
        attempts = 0
        for idx in order:
            if attempts >= attempt_limit:
                break
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 8.0:
                break
            idx = int(idx)
            cand = candidates[idx]
            # Avoid re-evaluating duplicates by exact bytes-ish tuple.
            key = (cand.size, tuple(np.round(cand, 12).astype(np.float64)) if cand.size <= 2048 else None)
            if key in evaluated:
                continue
            evaluated.add(key)
            attempts += 1
            ts = true_score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(ts) and ts < best_true_score:
                best_true_score = float(ts)
                best_true_a = cand.copy()
                best_fast = min(best_fast, fast_scores[idx])
                print(f"final candidate {idx}: length={cand.size}, true={best_true_score:.8f}")

    # Final safety check.
    if np.isfinite(best_true_score) and best_true_a is not None and best_true_a.size >= 1:
        final_a = normalize_pos(best_true_a)
    else:
        final_a = normalize_pos(best_a)

    # Ensure sum is safely above evaluator threshold and all values are finite/nonnegative.
    final_a = np.asarray(final_a, dtype=np.float64)
    final_a = np.where(np.isfinite(final_a), final_a, 0.0)
    final_a = np.maximum(final_a, 0.0)
    s = float(final_a.sum())
    if s < 0.05:
        final_a = final_a + 0.05
        s = float(final_a.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        final_a = final_a / s

    final_true = true_score(final_a)
    if np.isfinite(final_true) and final_true <= best_true_score + 1e-12:
        pass

    print(f"FINAL: length={final_a.size}, true={final_true:.8f}, best_tracked={best_true_score:.8f}")
    return [float(v) for v in final_a]
```
